- summary: PSOs must apply a minimum 12-hour cooling period before allowing online payment transactions after the registered mobile number or e-mail ID linked to the payment instrument is changed.
- country: India
- applies_to: non-bank payment system operators (PSOs); payment instruments; online payment channels
- legal_adjacent: True
- Applies to: FTR:FTR-001 (constrains), FTR:FTR-002 (constrains), FTR:FTR-003 (constrains), FTR:FTR-008 (constrains)

**UNVERIFIED** · compliance_note · impact high · confidence low · flags: checker_same_vendor, legal-adjacent, needs-confirm, partial_support, single-source · appendix (over_store_limit)

The PSO must identify remote access applications where possible and prohibit access to the mobile payment application while remote access is live.

- Source `S-071`: rbi.org.in (official, 2026-11-05) https://rbi.org.in/Scripts/BS_ViewMasDirections.aspx?id=13751
- topic: mobile payment security controls

- alternates: Choose automatic order timing (Yes) or manual customer instructions (No) for delivery timing.
- exceptions: You cannot offer both delivery types at the same location, but you can switch between them at any time.
